      "summary": "C++26 is bringing some long-overdue changes to&nbsp; assert() . But why are those changes needed? And when do we actually use&nbsp; assert , anyway? I&rsquo;m almost certain you&rsquo;ve used it before &mdash; at work, in personal projects, or at the very least in examples and code snippets. So what&rsquo;s the problem?
